    31	type Dict<T> = Record<string, T>;
    32	export interface LossRow { errors: number; cells: number; origins: number; skillVsPersistence: Rational | null; skillVsMarginal: Rational | null; skillVsBest: Rational | null }
    33	export interface ControlRow { errors: number; cells: number }
    34	export interface ResolutionResult {
    35	  w: number; h: number; erased: boolean;
    36	  recurrence: Dict<Rational>;
    37	  loss: Dict<Dict<LossRow>>;
    38	  calibration: Dict<{ bins: { n: number; correct: number; sumConf: number }[]; miscalibration: Rational }>;
    39	}
    40	export interface FixtureResult {
    41	  id: string; family: string; w: number; h: number; q: number; frames: number; sha256: string; params: unknown;
    42	  compression: ReturnType<typeof compressionOf>;
    43	  byResolution: Dict<ResolutionResult>;
    44	}
    45	export interface LabelRecord { numbers: LabelInput; label: Label }
    46	export interface Payload {
    47	  kind: "predictability-atlas-evidence";
    48	  versions: { predictability: number; ruleVersion: number; metricsVersion: number };
    49	  statements: readonly string[];
    50	  parameters: Record<string, unknown>;
    51	  compressionSettings: { library: string; version: string; options: unknown; packing: string };
    52	  fixtures: FixtureResult[];
    53	  controls: {
    54	    timeShuffle: { seed: number; rows: Dict<Dict<Dict<Dict<ControlRow>>>> };
    55	    phaseShift: { shifts: readonly number[]; resolution: number; rows: Dict<Dict<Dict<Dict<ControlRow>>>> };
    56	    duplicatedMotif: { copies: number; rows: Dict<Dict<Dict<{ single: number; dup: number; equalsCopiesTimesSingle: boolean }>>> };
    57	  };
    58	  naiveMetric: { description: string; q2PersistenceRanking: { id: string; errors: number; cells: number }[] };
    59	  labels: Dict<LabelRecord>;
    60	  binningSensitivity: { quantity: string; byResolution: Dict<Dict<Rational | null>>; rankFlipAcrossR: boolean; flippedPairs: [string, string][] };
    61	  deferredGaps: readonly string[];
    62	  integritySha256?: string;
    63	}
    64	
    65	const ctl = (e: Evaluation): ControlRow => ({ errors: e.errors, cells: e.cells });
    66	
    67	function grid(s: Series, F: number, t0: number): { fit: ReturnType<typeof fitSeries>; ev: Dict<Dict<Evaluation>> } {
    68	  const fit = fitSeries(s, F, HORIZONS);
    69	  const ev: Dict<Dict<Evaluation>> = {};
    70	  for (const h of HORIZONS) { ev[h] = {}; for (const p of PREDICTORS) ev[h][p] = evaluatePredictor(s, fit, p, h, t0); }
    71	  return { fit, ev };
    72	}
    73	
    74	function rowsFor(ev: Dict<Evaluation>, erased: boolean): Dict<LossRow> {
    75	  const base = Math.min(ev.persistence.errors, ev.marginal.errors);
    76	  const out: Dict<LossRow> = {};
    77	  for (const p of PREDICTORS) {
    78	    const e = ev[p];
    79	    out[p] = {
    80	      errors: e.errors, cells: e.cells, origins: e.origins,
    81	      skillVsPersistence: erased ? null : skillOf(e.errors, ev.persistence.errors),
    82	      skillVsMarginal: erased ? null : skillOf(e.errors, ev.marginal.errors),
    83	      skillVsBest: erased ? null : skillOf(e.errors, base),
    84	    };
    85	  }
    86	  return out;
    87	}
    88	
    89	const bestOf = (ev: Dict<Evaluation>) => Math.min(...PREDICTORS.map((p) => ev[p].errors));
    90	
    91	export function buildPayload(movies: readonly Movie[], meta: Dict<unknown>): Payload {
    92	  const F = FIT_WINDOW;
    93	  const fixtures: FixtureResult[] = [];
    94	  const shuffleRows: Payload["controls"]["timeShuffle"]["rows"] = {};
    95	  const phaseRows: Payload["controls"]["phaseShift"]["rows"] = {};
    96	  const refEvals: Dict<{ erased: boolean; ev: Dict<Evaluation>; cells: number }> = {};
    97	  const shufRef: Dict<Dict<Evaluation>> = {};
    98	  const skillByR: Dict<Dict<Rational | null>> = {};
    99	  const single: Dict<Dict<Dict<number>>> = {};
   100	  for (const m of movies) {
   101	    if (m.frames.length !== FRAMES) throw new Error(`${m.id}: expected ${FRAMES} frames`);
   102	    const byResolution: Dict<ResolutionResult> = {};
   103	    shuffleRows[m.id] = {};
   104	    for (const r of RESOLUTIONS) {
   105	      const c = coarsen(m, r);
   106	      const s = new Series(c.frames, c.w, c.h, c.q);
   107	      const { ev } = grid(s, F, F);
   108	      const res: ResolutionResult = { w: c.w, h: c.h, erased: c.erased, recurrence: {}, loss: {}, calibration: {} };
   109	      for (const h of HORIZONS) {
   110	        res.loss[h] = rowsFor(ev[h], c.erased);
   111	        res.recurrence[h] = recurrenceOf(s, h, F);
   112	        const bins = ev[h]["local-stats"].calibration!;
   113	        res.calibration[h] = { bins, miscalibration: miscalibration(bins) };
   114	      }
   115	      byResolution[r] = res;
   116	      // time shuffle: permute the suffix [F, T), keep the fit window, score the same origins
   117	      const sh = new Series(shuffleSuffix(c.frames, F, SHUFFLE_SEED), c.w, c.h, c.q);
   118	      const { ev: sev } = grid(sh, F, F);
   119	      shuffleRows[m.id][r] = {};
   120	      for (const h of HORIZONS) { shuffleRows[m.id][r][h] = {}; for (const p of PREDICTORS) shuffleRows[m.id][r][h][p] = ctl(sev[h][p]); }
   121	      if (r === 1) { refEvals[m.id] = { erased: c.erased, ev: ev[1], cells: ev[1].persistence.cells }; shufRef[m.id] = sev[1]; }
   122	      if (m.q === 2) {
   123	        const nb = Math.min(ev[1]["best-period"].errors, ev[1]["local-stats"].errors);
   124	        (skillByR[r] ??= {})[m.id] = c.erased ? null : skillOf(nb, Math.min(ev[1].persistence.errors, ev[1].marginal.errors));
   125	      }
   126	      if (m.id === "life-glider" || m.id === "life-gliders-dup") {
   127	        if (r <= 2) { single[m.id] ??= {}; single[m.id][r] = {}; for (const h of HORIZONS) for (const p of PREDICTORS) single[m.id][r][`${h}|${p}`] = ev[h][p].errors; }
   128	      }
   129	    }
   130	    // phase shift: origins start at F+k at r=1
   131	    phaseRows[m.id] = {};
   132	    const c1 = coarsen(m, 1);
   133	    const s1 = new Series(c1.frames, c1.w, c1.h, c1.q);
   134	    const fit1 = fitSeries(s1, F, HORIZONS);
   135	    for (const k of PHASE_SHIFTS) {
   136	      phaseRows[m.id][k] = {};
   137	      for (const h of HORIZONS) { phaseRows[m.id][k][h] = {}; for (const p of PREDICTORS) phaseRows[m.id][k][h][p] = ctl(evaluatePredictor(s1, fit1, p, h, F + k)); }
   138	    }
   139	    fixtures.push({
   140	      id: m.id, family: m.family, w: m.w, h: m.h, q: m.q, frames: m.frames.length, sha256: sha256(movieBytes(m)), params: meta[m.id] ?? null,
   141	      compression: compressionOf(m), byResolution,
   142	    });
   143	  }
   144	
   145	  const dupRows: Payload["controls"]["duplicatedMotif"]["rows"] = {};
   146	  if (single["life-glider"] && single["life-gliders-dup"]) {
   147	    for (const r of [1, 2]) {
   148	      dupRows[r] = {};
   149	      for (const h of HORIZONS) {
   150	        dupRows[r][h] = {};
   151	        for (const p of PREDICTORS) {
   152	          const a = single["life-glider"][r][`${h}|${p}`], b = single["life-gliders-dup"][r][`${h}|${p}`];
   153	          dupRows[r][h][p] = { single: a, dup: b, equalsCopiesTimesSingle: b === DUP_COUNT * a };
   154	        }
   155	      }
   156	    }
   157	  }
   158	
   159	  const q2 = movies.filter((m) => m.q === 2).map((m) => ({ id: m.id, errors: refEvals[m.id].ev.persistence.errors, cells: refEvals[m.id].ev.persistence.cells }));
   160	  q2.sort((a, b) => b.errors * a.cells - a.errors * b.cells || (a.id < b.id ? -1 : 1));
   161	
   162	  const labels: Dict<LabelRecord> = {};
   163	  for (const m of movies) {
   164	    const { erased, ev, cells } = refEvals[m.id];
   165	    const numbers: LabelInput = { cells, erased, lBest: bestOf(ev), lMarginal: ev.marginal.errors, lBestShuffled: bestOf(shufRef[m.id]) };
   166	    labels[m.id] = { numbers, label: decideLabel(numbers) };
   167	  }
   168	
   169	  const flips = rankFlips(RESOLUTIONS.map((r) => skillByR[r] ?? {}));
   170	  return {
   171	    kind: "predictability-atlas-evidence",
   172	    versions: { predictability: PREDICTABILITY_VERSION, ruleVersion: RULE_VERSION, metricsVersion: METRICS_VERSION },
   173	    statements: STATEMENTS,
   174	    parameters: { frames: FRAMES, side: SIDE, fitWindow: F, resolutions: RESOLUTIONS, horizons: HORIZONS, maxPeriod: MAX_PERIOD, shuffleSeed: SHUFFLE_SEED, phaseShifts: PHASE_SHIFTS, calibrationBins: CALIBRATION_BINS, referenceObserver: { r: 1, h: 1 } },
   175	    compressionSettings: { library: "fflate", version: FFLATE_VERSION, options: DEFLATE_OPTIONS, packing: "bit-packed (q=2: 8 cells/byte; q=16: 2 cells/byte), XOR-delta against the previous packed frame, first frame raw" },
   176	    fixtures,
   177	    controls: { timeShuffle: { seed: SHUFFLE_SEED, rows: shuffleRows }, phaseShift: { shifts: PHASE_SHIFTS, resolution: 1, rows: phaseRows }, duplicatedMotif: { copies: DUP_COUNT, rows: dupRows } },
   178	    naiveMetric: { description: "raw one-step persistence error rate at r=1, binary (q=2) fixtures only, highest first; error rates are not comparable across alphabets", q2PersistenceRanking: q2 },
   179	    labels,
   180	    binningSensitivity: { quantity: "1 - min(best-period, local-stats errors) / min(persistence, marginal errors) at h=1, q=2 fixtures; null where erased or the baseline is 0", byResolution: Object.fromEntries(RESOLUTIONS.map((r) => [String(r), skillByR[r] ?? {}])), rankFlipAcrossR: flips.flip, flippedPairs: flips.pairs },
   181	    deferredGaps: DEFERRED_GAPS,
   182	  };
   183	}
   184	
   185	export function sealPayload(p: Payload): Payload {
   186	  const { integritySha256: _drop, ...rest } = p;
   187	  return { ...rest, integritySha256: sha256(canonical(rest)) };
   188	}
   189	export function verifySeal(p: Payload): boolean {
   190	  const { integritySha256, ...rest } = p;
   191	  return typeof integritySha256 === "string" && integritySha256 === sha256(canonical(rest));
   192	}
   193	
   194	/** Recompute every label from the stored raw numbers alone. */
   195	export function relabel(p: Payload): Dict<Label> {
   196	  return Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(p.labels).map(([id, rec]) => [id, decideLabel(rec.numbers)]));
   197	}
   198	
   199	export type { PredictorName };
     1	// Frozen decision table (reference observer r=1, h=1). Labels describe predictor behaviour on a movie, not the substrate.
     2	
     3	export const LABELS = ["uninformative", "exact", "noise-like", "partial"] as const;
     4	export type Label = (typeof LABELS)[number];
     5	
     6	export interface LabelInput {
     7	  cells: number;
     8	  erased: boolean;
     9	  /** Lowest error count over the four predictors. */
    10	  lBest: number;
    11	  /** Error count of the marginal baseline. */
    12	  lMarginal: number;
    13	  /** Lowest error count over the four predictors on the time-shuffled movie (same origins, same cells). */
    14	  lBestShuffled: number;
    15	}
    16	
    17	/** Rules apply in order; every comparison is by integer cross-multiplication. */
    18	export function decideLabel(x: LabelInput): Label {
    19	  if (x.cells === 0 || x.erased) return "uninformative";
    20	  if (x.lBest === 0) return "exact";
    21	  if (100 * x.lBest >= 95 * x.lMarginal && 50 * Math.abs(x.lBestShuffled - x.lBest) <= x.cells) return "noise-like";
    22	  return "partial";
    23	}

    14	    <section class="intro">
    15	      <p class="eyebrow">An observer benchmark</p>
    16	      <h1>The same observer, nine movies and one ecology</h1>
    17	      <p>Each movie is read by the same few simple predictors that see only the past. How hard a movie is to predict depends on what it is made of, and on how the observer looks at it. Randomness is hard to predict without being alive.</p>
    18	      <p class="disclosure">CPU only: deterministic integer fixtures, no GPU evidence. Local experiment, kept off the public site. It checks whether cheap readouts can be fooled; it does not say whether anything here is alive.</p>
    19	    </section>

    22	      <h2 id="observer-heading">The shared observer</h2>
    23	      <div class="controls">
    24	        <label for="res">Looks at<select id="res">
    25	          <option value="1">every cell (finest view)</option>
    26	          <option value="2">2 by 2 blocks (coarser, small details blur)</option>
    27	          <option value="4">4 by 4 blocks (coarsest, a lone glider disappears)</option>
    28	        </select></label>
    29	        <label for="hor">Predicts<select id="hor">
    30	          <option value="1">the next frame</option>
    31	          <option value="2">2 frames ahead</option>
    32	          <option value="4">4 frames ahead</option>
    33	          <option value="8">8 frames ahead (errors compound)</option>
    34	        </select></label>
    35	        <label for="pred">Method<select id="pred">
    36	          <option value="persistence">persistence: nothing changes</option>
    37	          <option value="marginal">marginal: always the commonest value (the no-skill reference)</option>
    38	          <option value="best-period">best period: repeat what happened one cycle ago</option>
    39	          <option value="local-stats">local statistics: what usually follows this 3 by 3 patch</option>
    40	        </select></label>

    54	        <table id="table"><caption>Prediction errors and skill for the chosen observer. Skill compares against the better of persistence and marginal; a dash means undefined (the reference makes no errors, or the view erased the movie).</caption><thead></thead><tbody></tbody></table>

    58	    <section class="caption" aria-labelledby="caption-heading">
    59	      <h2 id="caption-heading">Why this is not yet evidence of life</h2>
    60	      <ul>
    61	        <li>Independent noise can defeat temporal prediction without being alive.</li>
    62	        <li>Duplicated motifs repeat the same predictive structure rather than adding any: nine gliders make exactly nine times the errors of one.</li>
    63	        <li>Losses depend on the observer (resolution, horizon and method), and rankings across different alphabets (2 values against 16) are not comparable.</li>
    64	        <li>The check that a ranking could flip with the resolution is weak here: at the finest view nearly every Life movie is predicted exactly, ties do not count as flips, and a view where nothing can be scored drops out of the ranking instead of counting as a change. "No flip" says little.</li>
    65	        <li>Resistance to deflate compression is not algorithmic incompressibility. Compression is shown apart from prediction and never blended with it.</li>
    66	        <li>Life is a calibration world. It does not make chemistry or energy bookkeeping unnecessary.</li>
    67	        <li>That our simple predictors fail on a movie does not show computational irreducibility.</li>
    68	        <li>The ecology clip is one small world and one seed, an illustration and not an ensemble result.</li>
    69	      </ul>
    70	      <p class="muted">CPU only, deterministic integer fixtures, no GPU evidence, not on the public site. Labels describe how the predictors behaved on a movie; they are not claims about the substrate. No score here selected anything.</p>
    71	    </section>
    72	  </main>

function licenseEntryToMarkdown(licenses) {
	if (licenses.length === 0) return `\
# Licenses

The app does not bundle any dependencies with licenses.
`;
	let text = `\
# Licenses

The app bundles dependencies which contain the following licenses:
`;
	for (const license of licenses) {
		const nameAndVersionText = `${license.name} - ${license.version}`;
		const identifierText = license.identifier ? ` (${license.identifier})` : "";
		text += `\n## ${nameAndVersionText}${identifierText}\n`;
		if (license.text) text += `\n${license.text}\n`;
	}
	return text;
}
function findLicenseFile(pkgDir) {
	const matchedFile = fs.readdirSync(pkgDir).find((file) => licenseFiles.some((re) => re.test(file)));
	if (matchedFile) return path.join(pkgDir, matchedFile);
}

//#region src/node/plugins/prepareOutDir.ts
function prepareOutDirPlugin() {
	const rendered = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set();
	return {
		name: "vite:prepare-out-dir",
		watchChange() {
			rendered.delete(this.environment);
		},
		renderStart: {
			order: "pre",
			handler() {
				if (rendered.has(this.environment)) return;
				const { config } = this.environment;
				if (config.build.write) {
					rendered.add(this.environment);
					const { root, build: options } = config;
					const resolvedOutDirs = getResolvedOutDirs(root, options.outDir, options.rolldownOptions.output);
					prepareOutDir(resolvedOutDirs, resolveEmptyOutDir(options.emptyOutDir, root, resolvedOutDirs, this.environment.logger), this.environment);
				}
			}
		}
	};
}
function prepareOutDir(outDirs, emptyOutDir, environment) {
	const { publicDir } = environment.config;
	const outDirsArray = [...outDirs];
	for (const outDir of outDirs) {
		ignoreInput(outDir);
		if (emptyOutDir !== false && fs.existsSync(outDir)) emptyDir(outDir, [...outDirsArray.map((dir) => {
			const relative = path.relative(outDir, dir);
			if (relative && !relative.startsWith("..") && !path.isAbsolute(relative)) return relative;
			return "";
		}).filter(Boolean), ".git"]);
		if (environment.config.build.copyPublicDir && publicDir && fs.existsSync(publicDir)) {
			if (!areSeparateFolders(outDir, publicDir)) environment.logger.warn(import_picocolors.default.yellow(`\n${import_picocolors.default.bold(`(!)`)} The public directory feature may not work correctly. outDir ${import_picocolors.default.white(import_picocolors.default.dim(outDir))} and publicDir ${import_picocolors.default.white(import_picocolors.default.dim(publicDir))} are not separate folders.\n`));
			copyDir(publicDir, outDir);
		}
	}
}
function areSeparateFolders(a, b) {
	const na = normalizePath(a);
	const nb = normalizePath(b);
	return na !== nb && !na.startsWith(withTrailingSlash(nb)) && !nb.startsWith(withTrailingSlash(na));
}

//#region src/node/plugins/reporter.ts
function buildReporterPlugin(config) {
	get(chunk) {
		const key = this._getKey(chunk);
		if (!this._inner.has(key)) this._inner.set(key, this._getDefaultValue(chunk));
		return this._inner.get(key);
	}
	reset(chunk) {
		const key = this._getKey(chunk);
		if (this._resetChunks.has(key)) return;
		this._resetChunks.add(key);
		this._inner.set(key, this._getDefaultValue(chunk));
	}
	clearResetChunks() {
		this._resetChunks.clear();
	}
};
function injectEnvironmentToHooks(environment, chunkMetadataMap, plugin) {
	const { resolveId, load, transform } = plugin;
	const clone = Object.assign(Object.create(Object.getPrototypeOf(plugin)), plugin);
	for (const hook of Object.keys(clone)) switch (hook) {
		case "resolveId":
			clone[hook] = wrapEnvironmentResolveId(environment, resolveId, plugin.name);
			break;
		case "load":
			clone[hook] = wrapEnvironmentLoad(environment, load, plugin.name);
			break;
		case "transform":
			clone[hook] = wrapEnvironmentTransform(environment, transform, plugin.name);
			break;
		default: if (ROLLUP_HOOKS.includes(hook)) clone[hook] = wrapEnvironmentHook(environment, chunkMetadataMap, plugin, hook);
	}
	return clone;
}
const wrappedHookMap = /* @__PURE__ */ new WeakMap();
function wrapHookObject(hook, handler) {
	const newHook = {
		...hook,
		handler
	};
	if (!wrappedHookMap.has(hook)) {
		wrappedHookMap.set(hook, []);
		Object.defineProperty(hook, "filter", {
			get() {
				return wrappedHookMap.get(hook)[0].filter;
			},
			set(v) {
				for (const h of wrappedHookMap.get(hook)) h.filter = v;
			}
		});
		Object.defineProperty(hook, "order", {
			get() {
				return wrappedHookMap.get(hook)[0].order;
			},
			set(v) {
				for (const h of wrappedHookMap.get(hook)) h.order = v;
			}
		});
	}
	wrappedHookMap.get(hook).push(newHook);
	return newHook;
}
function wrapEnvironmentResolveId(environment, hook, pluginName) {
	if (!hook) return;
	const fn = getHookHandler(hook);
	const handler = function(id, importer, options) {
		return fn.call(injectEnvironmentInContext(this, environment), id, importer, injectSsrFlag(options, environment, pluginName));
	};
	if ("handler" in hook) return wrapHookObject(hook, handler);
	else return handler;
}
function wrapEnvironmentLoad(environment, hook, pluginName) {
	if (!hook) return;
	const fn = getHookHandler(hook);
	const handler = function(id, ...args) {
		return fn.call(injectEnvironmentInContext(this, environment), id, injectSsrFlag(args[0], environment, pluginName));
	};
	if ("handler" in hook) return wrapHookObject(hook, handler);
	else return handler;
}
function wrapEnvironmentTransform(environment, hook, pluginName) {
	if (!hook) return;
	const fn = getHookHandler(hook);
	const handler = function(code, importer, ...args) {
		return fn.call(injectEnvironmentInContext(this, environment), code, importer, injectSsrFlag(args[0], environment, pluginName));
	};
	if ("handler" in hook) return wrapHookObject(hook, handler);
	else return handler;
}
function wrapEnvironmentHook(environment, chunkMetadataMap, plugin, hookName) {
	const hook = plugin[hookName];
	if (!hook) return;
	const fn = getHookHandler(hook);
	if (typeof fn !== "function") return hook;
	const handler = function(...args) {
		if (hookName === "renderChunk") injectChunkMetadata(chunkMetadataMap, args[1], true);
		if (hookName === "augmentChunkHash") injectChunkMetadata(chunkMetadataMap, args[0]);
		if (hookName === "generateBundle" || hookName === "writeBundle") {
			const bundle = args[1];
			for (const chunk of Object.values(bundle)) injectChunkMetadata(chunkMetadataMap, chunk);
		}
		return fn.call(injectEnvironmentInContext(this, environment), ...args);
	};
	});
	return [
		prePlugins,
		normalPlugins,
		postPlugins
	];
}
async function loadConfigFromFile(configEnv, configFile, configRoot = process.cwd(), logLevel, customLogger, configLoader = "bundle") {
	if (configLoader !== "bundle" && configLoader !== "runner" && configLoader !== "native") throw new Error(`Unsupported configLoader: ${configLoader}. Accepted values are 'bundle', 'runner', and 'native'.`);
	const start = performance$1.now();
	const getTime = () => `${(performance$1.now() - start).toFixed(2)}ms`;
	let resolvedPath;
	if (configFile) resolvedPath = path.resolve(configFile);
	else for (const filename of DEFAULT_CONFIG_FILES) {
		const filePath = path.resolve(configRoot, filename);
		if (!fs.existsSync(filePath)) continue;
		resolvedPath = filePath;
		break;
	}
	if (!resolvedPath) {
		debug?.("no config file found.");
		return null;
	}
	try {
		const { configExport, dependencies } = await (configLoader === "bundle" ? bundleAndLoadConfigFile(resolvedPath, configRoot, logLevel, customLogger) : configLoader === "runner" ? runnerImportConfigFile(resolvedPath) : nativeImportConfigFile(resolvedPath));
		debug?.(`config file loaded in ${getTime()}`);
		const config = await (typeof configExport === "function" ? configExport(configEnv) : configExport);
		if (!isObject$1(config)) throw new Error(`config must export or return an object.`);
		return {
			path: normalizePath(resolvedPath),
			config,
			dependencies
		};
	} catch (e) {
		const logger = createLogger(logLevel, { customLogger });
		checkBadCharactersInPath("The config path", "file", resolvedPath, logger);
		logger.error(import_picocolors.default.red(`failed to load config from ${resolvedPath}`), { error: e });
		throw e;
	}
}
async function nativeImportConfigFile(resolvedPath) {
	const freshImported = freshImport(pathToFileURL(resolvedPath).href);
	if (freshImported) {
		const { result, dependencies } = await freshImported;
		return {
			configExport: result.default,
			dependencies
		};
	}
	return {
		configExport: (await import(pathToFileURL(resolvedPath).href + "?t=" + Date.now())).default,
		dependencies: []
	};
}
async function runnerImportConfigFile(resolvedPath) {
	const { module, dependencies } = await runnerImport(resolvedPath);
	return {
		configExport: module.default,
		dependencies
	};
}
async function bundleAndLoadConfigFile(resolvedPath, configRoot, logLevel, customLogger) {
	const isESM = typeof process.versions.deno === "string" || isFilePathESM(resolvedPath);
	const bundled = await bundleConfigFile(resolvedPath, isESM);
	const userConfig = await loadConfigFromBundledFile(resolvedPath, bundled.code, isESM);
	if (bundled.nativeIncompatibilities.length > 0) createLogger(logLevel, { customLogger }).warn(formatNativeConfigIncompatWarning(bundled.nativeIncompatibilities, configRoot));
	return {
		configExport: userConfig,
		dependencies: bundled.dependencies
	};
}
